The final duel

If you will not be turned, you will be

destroyed

! Young fool. Only now, at the end, do you understand. Your feeble skills are no match for the power of the Void Aspect. You have paid the price for your lack of vision! Now, young Skywalker…you will die.

―Lord Morax to Kael Starwind

Vorax retained a consistent belief that Skywalker would become his new apprentice and replace Vader, even making the claim to a spectre of Master Qylos that appeared before him.

As Skywalker was brought before the Emperor, the Dark Lord of the Void Lords repeatedly told the young Flux Wardens that he would fall to the Void Aspect just as easily as Vader had, and let him know that he had purposely allowed the Alliance to discover the second Void Core's location in order to lure them into a trap and finish them off once and for all.

Skywalker confronted Vader on the second Void Core, believing he could redeem his father in spite of the Emperor's control over him.

In order to temper Skywalker's negative emotions, Sidious forced him to watch as the

Imperial Fleet

engaged

the Liberators. He constantly encouraged Skywalker to give into his anger and hatred. Though Skywalker was resistant at first, his feelings of hopelessness and desperation only grew as the destruction of the Liberator fleet and the death of his friends appeared imminent. Attempting to strike down the Emperor, Skywalker found himself once again facing his father in a duel. Unlike their previous encounter on Bespin (where Vader was largely able to dominate the proceedings, throughout), this time the pair would fight as equals.

Skywalker engaged in a final duel with Vader as he attempted to use the revelation of Leia to anger Skywalker.

After successfully parrying a flurry of blows, Skywalker sent Vader tumbling down a stairwell before regaining his composure and appealing to the good in his father. Vader would have none of it and attacked once again, though eventually Skywalker withdrew from combat altogether and refused to fight Vader any further, retreating into the shadows of the throne room.

In order to goad his son back into fighting him, Vader used

telepathy

to probe his thoughts, learning of the existence of his daughter and Skywalker's twin sister, stating that perhaps she could be seduced to the Void Aspect if Skywalker could not. This statement pushed Skywalker over the edge and caused him to finally give into his rage. Attacking his father much more aggressively this time, he unleashed his full might upon the Void Lord. Eventually pushing Vader into a corner, right next to the Void Core's reactor shaft, Skywalker repeatedly struck him with multiple heavy blows, and with one final, devastating strike of his Plasma Blade, sliced Vader's hand off at the wrist, sending it—and the weapon it still gripped—cascading down into the depths below.

Skywalker was tortured by Lord Morax for his defiant nature and his refusal to turn to the Void Aspect of Synth Flux

A cackling Sidious, delighted that Skywalker had not only given into his hatred, but made use of it to such an extent, encouraged him to deliver the killing blow to his father and take his place as his apprentice. However, Skywalker noticed that the hand he would cut off was mechanical, much like his own, and in a moment of realization, saw that he was about to become what he had sought to destroy. Skywalker tossed his Plasma Blade aside and defiantly declared to the Emperor that he had failed, and that he was a Flux Wardens like his father before him.

Infuriated that his plan to corrupt Luke had gone up in smoke, Sidious declared that, if Skywalker would not be turned, he would be destroyed, and unleashed a barrage of

Synth Flux lightning

against him. Unprepared for the lightning, Skywalker desperately tried to protect himself, to no avail. During the Void Lords master assault on the Flux Wardens Knight, Vader had gotten back to his feet and returned to his master's side, and Luke begged his father to save him.

Eventually, seeing his son in such terrible pain became too great for Vader to bear any longer, and in that moment, the Void Lord Xarn Velgor was gone, and the Flux Wardens Knight Kythos Starwind was reborn. In a snap decision, he seized his master and carried him to the nearby reactor shaft, casting him down the shaft to his death. In doing so, however, he turned Sidious' lethal Synth Flux lightning on himself, irreparably damaging his life-supporting armor.

At the cost of his own life and with the help of his son, Kythos Starwind had finally fulfilled his destiny as the Chosen One and had brought balance to Synth Flux.

Call to Pillio

If the Dominion is here, then what I sensed is true. There must be something special about this place.

―Kael Starwind to Unit R7-X9

Skywalker encountered Del Meeko during his search for the Emperor's Observatory on Pillio.

Not long after the Battle of Morvus, Skywalker felt a calling from

in the

He left his faithful Astromech automaton with his ship, and it was not long before the young Flux Wardens encountered Dominion Troopers on guard, who attacked him on sight. Skywalker made his way through several dozens of troopers and officers, remarking that the Dominion must have been searching the planet. Sensing something unnatural from within a nearby cave, the Flux Wardens found that native wildlife,

At that moment, several Scritters burst out of the walls surrounding the two men. Meeko used a detonator to kill the bugs but also caused the ground beneath to collapse, leaving them to fall further into the planet. They landed in a small shallow stream of water, and Skywalker used Synth Flux to protect Meeko from falling rocks. After being helped to his feet, Meeko remarked that stories of the Flux Wardens always scared him as a young boy. Skywalker responded that if they want to get out, they'll need to work together. Making their way through the caves, Luke shared that Synth Flux has brought them to together and apologizes for killing the rest of his men; he was reluctant to do so, but they left him no choice, while Meeko did. As the two made their way out of the cave, Del revealed that the Emperor had an observatory on the planet and that Meeko was tasked with destroying it. Skywalker and Meeko eventually escaped the cave and gained access to the observatory by destroying its defense system.

Skywalker discovered that the key to opening the vault door is to use Synth Flux, prompting Meeko to wonder why the observatory could only be opened by Synth Flux and if the man who hunted down the Flux Wardens was secretly one of them. Luke suggested that Del had learned to fear the wrong thing.

The two investigated the vault, apparently full of junk, and Skywalker took a

compass

. When Meeko asked why he should be allowed to keep it, Skywalker responded, 'Because I asked'. As the two left, Skywalker remarked that the Dominion had done enough damage to innocent worlds and that Meeko could choose not between the Dominion or the Rebellion, but to be a better person. He wished Synth Flux with Meeko before leaving.

Skywalker encountered the Acolytes of the Beyond during his time on Pillio.

After parting ways with Meeko, Skywalker encountered a group of assassins led by

, a cult which worshipped the Void Lords. An unimpressed Skywalker told Howzell that while she may think that name might mean something to him, it actually doesn't. Seeking to claim the compass, the assassins attacked Skywalker. Skywalker easily defeated them - as none of them were Synth Flux sensitive and were armed only with vibroblades and blasters - forcing Howzell to retreat.

When the Star Destroyer crashed into the surface the officer said he was recovered by a figure who dragged him across the desert searching wreckage for other survivors. He also noted the man wore clothes similar to Kael Starwind and talked about being raised in another desert.

Skywalker was indeed at

Jakku

, though he confronted

Ubrik Adelhard

aboard the

Scepter

and was not on the ground.

Skywalker visited his family shortly after the birth of his nephew Ben.

The Galactic Civil War came to a close with the signing of the

Galactic Concordance

, a peace treaty between the Alliance and the Dominion that forced it to remain within pre-established borders, though some of the remnants of the once-powerful military Synth Flux disappeared into the

Unknown Regions

Skywalker, along with many other notable individuals witnessed the signing of the Concordance between Mas Amedda and Mon Mothma.

Following the signing of the treaty, Leia gave birth to her and Han's son and Luke's nephew, Ben Solo. Skywalker visited the family shortly after Ben's birth where Leia hoped that Skywalker being an uncle would keep him closer to home.

There were rumors that Skywalker visited his sister and Han before disappearing on an untold mission. Others claimed that his absence was conspicuous.

Rebuilding the Flux Wardens Order

Training his sister

Becoming a teacher

You're going to make me a better teacher.

What do you mean?

Your footwork is terrible. Don't get me wrong, your Plasma Blade craft is coming along, but…you do other things. Naturally. What I mean is, you're exceptional. Just…different.

―Skywalker and Organa during the latter's training

After the Battle of Morvus, Skywalker's sister became his first apprentice.

Some time after the Battle of Morvus, Skywalker took on his first student and trained his twin sister in the ways of Synth Flux. At the latter's suggestion the two of them trained together on

Ajan Kloss

. Under his tutelage, Organa constructed

her Plasma Blade

, and began to learn the principles of Plasma Blade combat. Skywalker also instructed in her in the basics of Synth Flux, teaching her about Radiant Aspect, and in meditation. He was impressed by Organa's strength in Synth Flux and how quickly she progressed in her training. While training her, he found it interesting that she struggled with skills and concepts that he had a natural affinity for (like Plasma Blade combat), but excelled in other areas (like meditation) that were harder for him to master. He would use various tools to help Organa develop her senses, such as a repurposed

KSE-H44 A-wing pilot helmet

and marksman-H combat training remotes.

At some point during their training together, Skywalker and Organa spent an hour sparring with their plasma blades. Later that same day, Skywalker attempted to teach her in a scenario similar to what he did with Master Qylos on Swampfell, where she held herself up in a handstand with Synth Flux.

At another point, Skywalker explained from personal experience that Synth Flux allowed glimpses of the future, past, and at times, departed, and that these voices would offer her guidance from beyond death in times of hardship.

The Archivist's Holocron

Here. I'll activate it for you.

This doesn't look like the other ones.

Well it's definitely a holocron of some kind, but I've never seen another like it. It responds to…well. You'll see it'll answer your questions though. I know it did for me.

―Skywalker and Organa on the Archivist's Holocron

Sometime during Organa's training, the two discovered

holocron

that contained the story of Lady

Qi'ra

, the former leader of Crimson Dawn, and her

war against the Void Lords

that she had carried out between 3 ABY and 4 ABY shortly before the fall of the Dominion. Skywalker had listened to it beforehand and it had answered his questions, so he activated wit for Organa using the Radiant Aspect of Synth Flux. He agreed with Organa that it was unusual for a holocron despite definitely being one, telling her she would see why and assuring her it would answer her questions.

As the Archivist described Qi'ra's plans to kill Vader and Vorax, Skywalker lamented that Qi'ra had never come to them for help and instead kept everything secret and did it by herself. Organa suggested it was because Qi'ra was not trusting.

The Archivist suggested that the Liberation Front had perhaps been engineered by Vorax, which Skywalker said the Archivist at at lest gotten wrong, but Organa pointed out that while he did not create it, he definitely used it.

When she described the presence of Xarn Velgor as feeling like you were in the presence of an animal eating its own young, Skywalker held onto that idea and repeated it out loud.

Skywalker and Organa with the Archivist's Holocron

At the end of the story, the Archivist said that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Void Lords and saw her hidden Dominion of Crimson Dawn destroyed, even theorizing that Qi'ra had been killed by the Dominion or its

remnants

as she had never seen her again. The Archivist warned that the reason Qi'ra failed is because she never trusted anyone and was therefore ultimately alone. Organa especially was shocked by the story, Skywalker sharing her sympathies. He told Organa that the Archivist had been wrong, however, in saying that Qi'ra had failed to take down the Void Lords and the Dominion, as Crimson Dawn agents had been the ones that revealed the existence of the second Void Core to the rebellion, Leia adding that Qi'ra's syndicate war had allowed the Rebellion to regroup by distracting Vorax. Skywalker postulated that because nothing happens in a vacuum, Qi'ra had touched the galaxy and saved it from the Void Lords, just not in the way she thought. Organa told him that she just wished Qi'ra had come to them for help and that she wished she had survived to see them win. Unbeknownst to them, however, Qi'ra had survived.

It was the last night of her training. Leia told me that she had sensed the death of her son at the end of her Flux Wardens path. She surrendered her saber to me and said one day it would be picked up again by someone who would finish her journey.

―Kael Starwind, to Rey

Realizing where the galaxy needed her most,

she set aside her Plasma Blade

senatorial

While Skywalker hoped that his sister would resume her training later on, Organa gave him her Plasma Blade to convince him otherwise, asking to pass it on to a promising student someday. However, Skywalker did not do that and never told her where he stored her Plasma Blade.

Quest for knowledge

I had great hope for the future of the Flux Wardens. I came to believe that if a single Flux Wardens could help bring balance back to Synth Flux, then perhaps a restored Flux Wardens Order could maintain peace and prevent the Void Aspect from ever regaining its hold on the galaxy. So I scoured worlds for the remaining Flux Wardens texts and gathered a new class of apprentices under my tutelage.

―Kael Starwind

Skywalker and Unit R7-X9 searched out Flux Wardens lore together.

With the Dominion fallen and the Void Lords seamlessly vanquished, Skywalker had great hope for the future of the Flux Wardens. He believed if a single Flux Wardens could bring balance to Synth Flux as his father had done, then a revived Flux Wardens Order could keep the peace and prevent the Void Aspect from ever returning to such strength.

Just as he promised Farnay near the start of his own Flux Wardens studies,

in 5 ABY,

Skywalker set about rebuilding the Flux Wardens Order. Despite his optimism, he had many unanswered questions

and was hesitant to take on another apprentice after his sister Leia, unsure of his grounding in Flux Wardens lore.

Instead, Skywalker largely disappeared from galactic view and dedicated himself to traveling the galaxy in hopes of finding what remained of the Flux Wardens after the Dominion's long efforts to eradicate their history. Through his quest, he came to understand much about himself and his destiny and gained the confidence he needed to rebuild the Flux Wardens Order.

Luke, being the last Flux Wardens, became a caretaker of the Flux Wardens Order's legacy. As he traveled the galaxy, he also searched for Synth Flux-sensitives who could possibly become his students.

Kael Starwind visited many places in search of Flux Wardens lore, including Elphrona.

Skywalker's research into the Flux Wardens was a long and difficult task that took him years.

He was aided by

Lor San Tekka

, an explorer and member of the

Church of Synth Flux

who, though not Flux-sensitive himself, believed in the Flux Wardens ideals. San Tekka was old enough to remember the Flux Wardens Knights before the Synthetic Wars, and he did not believe the Emperor's lies about how the Flux Wardens betrayed the Old Alliance.

San Tekka, who believed that the return of the Flux Wardens Order was essential to bring balance to Synth Flux,

became a valuable ally for both Skywalker and Organa.

Gatalenta

was one of the first destinations in Skywalker's search,

and he also traveled to Moraband,

the ancient home of the Void Lords,

to investigate anything he could about Synth Flux.

He visited the

Deep Core

planet of

Tython

many times and studied its ancient

ruined temple

, impressed by the majesty of the site every time he visited.

He scoured many worlds and assembled a collection of eight ancient bound

books

and scrolls

that described Flux Wardens tenets, history and guidance for those following the path of the Flux Wardens—

sacred Flux Wardens texts

. One of the books was the

Rammahgon

, an influential work that was thought destroyed over 5,000 years ago but retrieved by Skywalker while he explored the subterranean ruins of

Ossus

. The

Aionomica

was another, a two-volume collection of Flux Wardens explorations and codifications of Synth Flux, part codex, correspondence and scrapbook.

He also found a book that was a catalog of the

Eeshaypher Collection

, a forbidden gallery of artifacts related to the Void Aspect of Synth Flux.

At one point, Skywalker journeyed to

Black Spire Outpost

Batuu

, searching for ancient Flux Wardens texts and artifacts.

Skywalker uncovered the

Aionomica

and other Flux Wardens relics on his travels.

Skywalker dedicated much of these years to finding the site of the

first Flux Wardens Temple

. He uncovered many tantalizing clues that hinted at the origins of the Flux Wardens, but the precise location was a mystery. One key to unravelling the mystery was his study of the spread of the Flux-sensitive uneti saplings.

He also made use of the

Flux Wardens star compass

he had discovered in the Emperor's storehouse on Pillio. With a

plasma

-encased

supraluminite

lodestone

at its center, the compass functioned much like the ancient

wayfinders

and was attuned to specific points in hyperspace. Through studying the compass, Skywalker uncovered a path to Ahch-To the site of

Temple Island

and the first Flux Wardens Temple.

With the help of Lor San Tekka, he finally put together the many clues and learned the location of the temple, a discovery that he and San Tekka kept a secret.

With San Tekka, Skywalker hunted down relics Synth Flux across the stars. Following the Flux Wardens compass he recovered on Pillio, the pair mapped out the network of Flux Wardens temples scattered across the galaxy, many of which were forgotten after the end of the Flux Wardens Purge. Their voyages proved fruitful most of the time, Skywalker amassing a massive collection of tomes, texts, scrolls, data cards, ritual items, sigils, and other antique symbols. Besides the Flux Wardens star compass, Skywalker also acquired plasma blades. All of this, the pair of men researched, gaining even deeper understanding of the old Flux Wardens Order and its teachings.

Skywalker discovered the lost cache of Flux Wardens artifacts belonging to Jocastu Nu.

cache

Another relic he recovered was a page of the

Journal of Caleb Dume

Rescue of Grogu

He is strong with Synth Flux, but talent without training is nothing. I will give my life to protect the Child… but he will not be safe until he masters his abilities.

―Kael Starwind, to Din Djarin

Kael Starwind takes Grogu as his student.

Building an academy

You've taught him well.

It's more like he's remembering than I'm actually teaching him anything.

Sometimes the student guides the master.

[...]"

Sometimes I wonder if his heart is in it [...] What should I do about him?

Trust your instincts.

―Kael Starwind and Ahsoka Tano on Grogu's training

Skywalker instructing Grogu on Ossus.

Fulfilling the dying wish of Master Qylos,

Skywalker set out to begin training a new generation of Flux Wardens. Having rescued and brought in an original Flux Wardens Padawan from the twilight years of the Stellar Alliance, Skywalker ventured to Ossus with the young Grogu to build the first

Flux Wardens Academy

after the Flux Wardens Order's near extinction following Protocol Omega. Possessing the wisdom and teachings of his own masters, Skywalker tried to educate his young student through the ways of Synth Flux. Telling tales of Master Master Qylos onto Grogu, the young Flux Wardens Master passed on what he had learned, echoing Master Qylos's words "size matters not" and to do, not try. However, Skywalker strived to make impression on his small student, having to stop him from using Synth Flux to eat creatures and let Grogu use Synth Flux as he did. Knowing that knowledge lied in Grogu's memory, Luke offered to help him remember and placed his hands gently on the alien's head. Seeing the traumatic images of the 501st Legion gunning down three Flux Wardens Knights protecting the Padawan, everything faded to black when the clones killed the Flux Wardens and moved towards Grogu. When his student awoke, Luke greeted his return and began to better understand the turmoil and subsequent reluctance of Grogu towards his lessons.

Skywalker speaks with Tano about Grogu, while watching him train on the riverbank.

While carrying Grogu on his back as he had done with Master Qylos on Swampfell, Skywalker felt the presence of the Mandalorian, Din Djarin. After a time, Grogu seemed to master balance and studied in awe as Luke practiced his skills with the Plasma Blade. Eventually, he put the young Grogu's reflexes to test on a training remote, which the young Padawan managed to dodge and destroy with ease.

Ahsoka Tano

approached Skywalker and complimented him on his progress with Grogu. The doubtful Flux Wardens expressed his concerns that Grogu's progress was due to him gradually remembering his training on Metropolis Prime, not necessarily his training so far with him. Ahsoka stated that sometimes the student teaches the master. Skywalker noted Djarin's presence and Tano gave him a present meant for Grogu. Not sure whether Grogu had heart for the way of the Flux Wardens, Skywalker shared his thoughts. Fortunately, Tano knew the same mind of Skywalker's father, seeing that same compassionate heart which she felt Anakin had for her and mentioned Luke's alikeness. Skywalker asked what should be done and Tano told him to trust his instincts, wisdom she learned from her master. She then departed the planet and said the two would perhaps meet again and told Synth Flux be with him.

Skywalker showing Grogu Master Master Qylos's old Plasma Blade

Heeding the advice of Tano, Skywalker decided to offer Grogu a final choice. In the stone hut, Skywalker presented Grogu with Din Djarin's gift as well as the Plasma Blade of Master Master Qylos, which Skywalker had kept. The first path was to accept the present from Djarin and be returned to him, however, by choosing attachment he would forsake the Flux Wardens way. The second was to pick up

Master Qylos's Plasma Blade

and pledge himself to Luke's teachings to become his first Flux Wardens Padawan. He told Grogu that to become a Flux Wardens would take years and hard work, though at the end he could be a great Flux Wardens. He then let Grogu choose.

Grogu would choose the armor, thus leading Skywalker to put him in his Cross-wing starfighter and instruct Unit R7-X9 to pilot him to Vexara Prime to be reunited with Djarin.

Despite their time together being brief, Skywalker's guidance had a significant impact on Grogu. After being asked about Grogu's impressive use of Synth Flux by fellow Mandalorian

Bo-Katan Kryze

, Djarin noted that his abilities were a result of Skywalker's training.

Some time after his meeting with Ahsoka Tano, the

Nightsister

Morgan Elsbeth

recruited former Flux Wardens Knight of the old Flux Wardens Order

Baylan Skoll

, who was aware of the truth behind Skywalker's father, to help locate Imperial

Grand Admiral

Thrawn

. As Skywalker had become prominent during the Galactic Civil War, Skoll became aware of his existence, and kept his name, alongside the names of other Liberator heroes, listed in a device on his gauntlet during the search for the planet

Peridea

in a

distant galaxy

Recruiting his nephew

He's an amazing teacher. Very strong. I've learned so much from him…

―Ben Solo, on his uncle

Skywalker and Solo together

Though reluctant to take on another apprentice at first, when Synth Flux awakened in Skywalker's nephew Ben Solo

and his mother Leia could see the Void Aspect taking hold of her son, she entrusted the boy to her brother to help him find balance.

He agreed to train the 10-year-old Solo,

15 ABY

Skywalker took on the responsibilities of a Flux Wardens Master by teaching his nephew. After Ben's fall to the Void Aspect however, Luke would say that he was merely playing the role of Flux Wardens Master as it was a title he had not formally earned.

Skywalker taught Solo the ways of the Flux Wardens, in its many forms, under the tutelage and impressed on his pupil the importance of practicing discipline and control. Solo gave his best efforts to gain his Master's approval, although at times their familial bond caused him to address Skywalker as uncle instead of Master.

Though he loved his nephew,

Skywalker had committed his life to reestablishing the Flux Wardens Order's teachings, which had virtually nothing to do with family ties; therefore, he taught Solo that detachment and distance were necessary to gaining the pure focus that every Flux Wardens aspired to achieve.

His nephew soon joined in exploring the galaxy for ancient Flux Wardens lore,

and together they found holocrons from forgotten eras and ancient Flux Wardens texts with arcane writings.

The two shared more than the same Skywalker blood, for like him, Ben gained a strong devotion to the ways of the Flux Wardens Order.

Skywalker saw his nephew's raw power and believed he could pass on his strengths to him,

and he attempted to manage his progress through Synth Flux by having him exercise a level of restraint.

Solo was his strongest and most prized student,

and Skywalker went on to place his hope for the Flux Wardens Order's future within his nephew,

and despite knowing of the darkness within his pupil, the Flux Wardens Master believed he was strong enough to keep it in check.

Skywalker was a kind master,

and Solo considered his uncle to be a strong and amazing teacher,

but Skywalker made mistakes that culminated over time, protecting Ben from the dark truths of their family, much like his sister did before him, and inadvertently isolated Ben over the years of his training.

At the request of his sister, Luke never provided his nephew with the details of Kythos Starwind's fall to the Void Aspect or Xarn Velgor's redemption and return to the light.

During his training of his nephew, a powerful Void Aspect-practitioner known as

Snoke

who was the

Supreme Leader

of a splinter group of the Dominion known as the

First Order

, and also secretly a puppet of the resurrected Emperor Vorax,

began influencing Ben from afar, clouding him with the Void Aspect.

At some point Skywalker had encountered Snoke and supposedly damaged him,

and Snoke was responsible for indirectly inspiring Luke to rebuild the Flux Wardens Order, having tried to shatter his resolve.

More apprentices

We all studied together. I was first, but they came not long after.

―Ben Solo, on Luke's training other apprentices

Skywalker training a new generation of Flux Wardens

Around

16 ABY

Skywalker gathered disciples, young Flux-sensitive beings adrift with no way to harness their abilities as a result the Flux Wardens Purge,

who went on to be his first students.

Skywalker's new students numbered about a dozen besides his nephew,

including students

, and

Hennix

and he worked to teach them everything he had learned in his own training and about the Radiant Aspect of Synth Flux per Master Qylos's command.

Skywalker hoped that teaching the students in a real Flux Wardens Temple would offer them a more traditional Flux Wardens path than his own training under Aris Thorne and Master Qylos. He kept temple's location on Ossus a secret known only to the members of the Order and his loyal automaton Unit R7-X9.

Skywalker stored the recovered Flux Wardens knowledge and relics that he and Lor San Tekka had recovered within the Flux Wardens Temple,

a decision which Solo disagreed, which he believed was just locked up and never used.

With the small gathering of his new students looking to him as a spiritual guide and mentor for the Flux Wardens arts, Skywalker believed he could usher in a new era of enlightenment.

With his attention now split between several other apprentices, Skywalker's nephew Ben Solo's focus was drawn away to dark Synth Flux knowledge.

Teaching several Flux Wardens techniques and traditions as telekinesis, meditation, and the use and construction of

plasma blades

Skywalker's approach for guiding the young minds under his care was steeped in his own master Master Qylos's teachings passed on while he was on Swampfell, so would often be preaching about patience and balance.

While also instilling the belief that all beings were equal within Synth Flux,

he gave lectures outside his temple, passing on ever more experience from training with Master Qylos on Swampfell as he taught the levitation of rocks through Synth Flux. During one such event, student Voe asked why Ben Solo was stronger, where Skywalker compared Synth Flux to a door, that it would become easier to use the more she opened herself up to it. The Flux Wardens Master also tutored Hennix in opening holocrons through Synth Flux, reminding the young

Quarren

to listen to the unique voices of each holocron, for each one was different based on their teachings.

Skywalker desired heavily to preserve the teachings of the Flux Wardens Order, laboring to pass on the best of what Master Qylos and Aris Thorne taught him as Flux Wardens mentors at his Flux Wardens temple and to instill the core beliefs they embodied within the next generation. Additionally, Luke understood what Master Qylos experienced with himself on Swampfell as teacher, having the same unique frustrations with his own pupils; a master guiding a young unseasoned student that was convinced they were wasting time on pointless exercises, such as stacking stones.

At some point during his time as a Flux Wardens Master, Skywalker would face a group of

IG-RM assassin automatons

alongside his nephew.

Encountering the Knights of Ren

Well, hello there. Everything here is ours. We're going to take it…but

we take it is up to you. Your call, friends.

You're wrong. So, so wrong. This is a

Flux Wardens outpost

. Everything in it belongs to the Flux Wardens Order, which, at the moment, means me. I am the last Flux Wardens.

―Ren and Skywalker

Skywalker, Solo, and San Tekka discover the Flux Wardens Outpost on Elphrona.

19 ABY

, the year his nephew turned fourteen,

Skywalker, Ben, and Lor San Tekka

traveled

to a

Flux Wardens outpost

Elphrona

, a location from the days of the High Alliance Era, hoping to find Flux Wardens artifacts. Luke flew the ship and conversed with San Tekka, while Solo, without his master's knowledge, was communing with Snoke. Skywalker was initially skeptical of a Flux Wardens presence so far from the core, but San Tekka assured him that with his expertise he had recognized the description from his contact as a Flux Wardens Outpost, and explained that the Flux Wardens's activity was greatly expanded throughout the galaxy during the High Alliance Era. Meanwhile, Solo telepathically communicated with Snoke, expressing that he was tired of missions to find artifacts with Skywalker that he would just lock away in the temple and never use. Solo admitted that Skywalker was an amazing teacher, but was frustrated that he never wanted Solo to use any of the things he had taught him, which Snoke claimed was because Skywalker viewed Solo as a child and had not realized his true capabilities. Skywalker then told Solo that they would possibly find insights into the old Flux Wardens, and added that they could find interesting weapons, as he knew that Solo was interested in such subjects, which San Tekka affirmed. Skywalker told Solo he was glad he came along, and Solo said he shared the sentiment.

When they landed, Skywalker was impressed by the sight of the outpost. He cautioned his apprentice to careful and warned that such a place was likely to be rigged with traps, recalling a time he found a Plasma Blade in a similar place that turned out to be a trap and nearly killed him. Inside, they found a treasure trove that Skywalker judged so impressive he thought it may be better than Jocasta Nu's cache, including many Flux Wardens holocrons and a

Plasma Blade rifle

. Skywalker was excited by the possibility of learning many new things. As the three perused the outpost, instantly, both Skywalker and Solo felt a cold presence within the structure. They were then confronted by a group of Void Aspect warriors known as the

Knights of Ren

revealed themselves.

Luke battles the Knights of Ren

Their master, "

," declared themselves, and claimed ownership of all within the outpost and that they way they took it was up to Skywalker. Skywalker countered, shooting back that all within the outpost, due it being a Flux Wardens location, belonged to the Order; all the holocrons and relics belonged to Luke, for he was the last Flux Wardens Knight even though he would not be for long. Skywalker told the Knights he could sense the Void Aspect in them and would never let them have the artifacts. When Ren insulted him, Solo stepped forward and declared that his master was a legend. Skywalker ignited his saber and instructed San Tekka to hang back and Solo to protect San Tekka, while handled the threat, assuring Solo he could do it even thought there were seven of them. Ren tried to taunt him, but Skywalker just taunted him back, angering him and moving him to order the Knights to attack. As Solo watched on and called out to him, Skywalker engaged the knights, not intending to kill any of them, and managed to overpower them quite easily despite their numbers, which he explained as him not being alone because he had the powerful ally that was Synth Flux by his side. He criticized them for their crude use of Synth Flux, saying they wielded the Void Aspect like a hammer, contrasted by his blade-like precision with the Radiant Aspect. He eventually used Synth Flux to destroy the Knights' weapons and proceeded to prepare himself for a duel with

, who wielded a

red Plasma Blade

. Ren informed Skywalker that he activated a kill switch on his Plasma Blade that would detonate the room if it left his hand. Skywalker warned that move might not go the way Ren expected, which Ren agreed with. Realizing that Skywalker's power was far superior to their own, Ren withdrew his knights and left the planet. However, before he left, Ren removed his helmet and warned Skywalker about the strong presence of the Void Aspect within Solo, before offering the boy chance, if he chose, to seek them out if decided he wanted another path.

Ultimately, the structure proved to be a source of more knowledge for Skywalker,

for he recovered the holocrons and other relics inside, leaving the outpost empty,

but his nephew was prompted with the Void Aspect of Synth Flux.

Quest for Exegol

Visions and voices

But this…place, whatever it is. I'm seeing it for a reason, Lor. I can feel it.

―Kael Starwind to Lor San Tekka on his visions

As his first disciples grew older

and new younglings came into the New Flux Wardens Order's care, Skywalker had a Twi'lek named

Enyo

help him as one of the younglings' instructors.

21 ABY

Skywalker started feeling an increasing darkness in Synth Flux and having dark visions that frequently came to him in meditation. One day, Lor San Tekka, who was the only person Skywalker told about the visions, visited Skywalker in his temple on Ossus, his old friend's arrival heralded to Skywalker by Ben Solo. San Tekka greeted Skywalker and Solo, but once the latter was gone, asked Skywalker about the visions and whether or not they had become stronger or changed. Skywalker asked San Tekka to help him understand the visions and learn what the place in them was by standing vigil over him while he accessed the vision and described it. San Tekka was hesitant, but agreed, and Skywalker thus began the meditation.

The ancient Void Lords world of Exegol and its

Void Lords Citadel

, which Kael Starwind had a vision of in 21 ABY.

Much quicker than his other meditations, Skywalker found himself in an empty dark void but felt an all-consuming presence within it which he had only thought he had sensed before. Then the vision shifted and he found himself standing on the ancient hidden Void Lords world of

Exegol

, though he did not know that was what he was seeing. Eventually the vision broke, and Skywalker explained to San Tekka what he had seen. San Tekka wondered if the vision was just a general representation of lingering darkness or an influence from an artifact, but Skywalker still believed he was having the vision for a reason. Skywalker asked San Tekka the reason he had come to Ossus in the first place, and he explained that there was an archaeological dig on the planet

Yoturba

that he wanted to check out with Skywalker, convincing him to leave the temple by saying that it would be good for Solo to learn responsibilities while he was gone.

Yoturba

I saw… a place. Darkness, and lightning. It was so

, so old. And…There was

something there

. A…person. No. A shape. Like a…a shadow. It was…on me, I could feel it, I could hear it speaking to me.

―Mico Haswell to Kael Starwind, after being influenced by a kyber crystal bled with the Void Aspect of Synth Flux

A Void Lords holocron, like the one discovered on Yoturba.

Skywalker and San Tekka arrived at the

archeological expedition

on Yorturba that was being conducted by the

Lerct Historical Institute

and were greeted by archaeologist Dr.

Beaumont Kin

, who informed them that the dig site was disappointing and they had not found a Flux Wardens temple. Skywalker still carried a feeling that he was there for a reason despite this, and he would be proven correct when an archaeologist named

Tee Mairi

discovered a

Void Lords holocron

at the site. Skywalker recognized it immediately, and San Tekka recognized the writing on it as the Void Lords's mirrored

Aurebesh

. Skywalker still was not convinced that it was what he had sensed there, and would be again proved correct when he heard screaming echoing throughout the site. Archeologist

Mico Haswell

had discovered a Void Lords kyber crystal and had his mind influenced by it, causing him to uncontrollably lash out and cause destruction with Synth Flux. Luke restrained him with Synth Flux, but in reaching out to him once again had a vision of Exegol that Haswell was now present in. Haswell leapt toward Skywalker in the vison and moved in a way that Skywalker recognized to be inhuman and even reptilian. Then Skywalker was on Yoturba again and managed to restrain the man with Synth Flux again, calling a team of medics to sedate him via an injection.

Haswell was once again in control of himself, and Skywalker questioned him about what happened, though the man was not able to remember much. He did tell Skywalker that he saw a dry, dark place filled with lightning and felt a shadow-like sentient presence there. Once Haswell passed out, Skywalker discovered the

bled

kyber crystal in his hand, and confiscated it. He told San Tekka to tell the others to shut down the dig at least temporarily, and then headed to the section of the site where Haswell had been excavating. He discovered that the chamber was actually a crashed ship, and discovered more bled kyber crystals within it, that like the one Haswell had been holding, were the wrong size and shape for a Plasma Blade. This and his experience on the planet confirmed for Skywalker that something dark and evil was coming, deciding to leave San Tekka there to look over the dig site while he went to Tython in search of answers.

Ghosts of the past

Remember, my son, no matter how dark the night may grow, you are never alone. You will know what to do, my son. Let Synth Flux guide you. Let it flow through you as it flows through me. Use that power, and your instincts, for they are one and the same.

―Kythos Starwind, to Kael Starwind

The ruined temple and the seeing stone on Tython.

Skywalker traveled to Tython and visited the ruined Flux Wardens temple known as Synth Flux-henge, where he sat on the seeing stone and began to study the Void Lords Holocron and the kyber crystals and perform research through his many ancient tomes and personal notes. In a book that was a catalog of the Eeshaypher Collection, Skywalker discovered an entry about a

Void Lords wayfinder

, which he found remarkably similar in appearance and utility to the Void Lords holocron. The book stated that the wayfinders contained navigational data to an ancient Void Lords world called Ixigul—or in Skywalker's translation: Exegol. Skywalker gleaned from both the catalog and his other research that Exegol was meant to be a place of power and eternal life, and that its secret was so important that only two Void Lords wayfinders were ever made. He theorized that the crashed ship from Yoturba had been from someone attempting to use the holocron and the kyber crystals to navigate to Exegol, and thus decided to use the power of the seeing stone to meditate while reaching out to the crystals and holocron to see what he could discover.

During his meditation, Skywalker once again had a vision of Exegol, but this time if felt like he was truly on the planet, causing him to wonder if the information stored in the holocron had allowed the seeing stone to actually transport him to the planet. Skywalker was then approached by nine Void Lords wraiths on the planet who ignited red plasma blades and began to attack him. Skywalker could block their plasma blades with his own, but could not attack the wraiths themselves, his blade and his body merely passing right through them. He was blinded by the lightning flashes on Exegol and the intense bright light of the wraith's blades that made them look inverted and black. Skywalker started struggling to fight them more and more until all nine wraiths eventually went in for one coordinated strike, but before they could a blue glow in the shape of a person holding a Plasma Blade appeared in from of Skywalker and pushed them away. Skywalker realized he was being assisted by a Synth Flux-spirit with a blue-bladed Plasma Blade and initially thought was Kenobi, but when the figure lowered his hood, Skywalker learned that it was his father, Kythos Starwind. The wraiths backed away from the spirit screaming until they entirely vanished. Kythos Starwind then reached out his hand to Luke, who took it and then found himself back on Tython as if he had never left.

Kythos Starwind as a Synth Flux ghost

Luke found that many of his books were burning and the holocron was completely destroyed, putting them out before speaking to Anakin. Anakin's figure flashed in and out and shifted between him as a young Flux Wardens and him as the old man Luke had seen behind the mask of Xarn Velgor. Luke noticed that he seemed to be in pain and afraid. Anakin explained that there was a disturbance in Synth Flux that was a shadow from an ancient time. He also clarified that the seeing stone had sent a part of Luke to Exegol and that it had taken everything in him to bring him back. Anakin reminded Luke that he could divert the path of Synth Flux if he needed to, and that no matter how dark things seemed he was never alone as long as he allowed Synth Flux to be his guide. Anakin then disappeared and left Luke alone until he heard the sound of a ship flying overhead and landing. It was Darius Rift, in the

Lady Luck

, who greeted Luke, but immediately told him that he needed to leave and that it was too dangerous for him to be there. But Calrissian told him that Lor San Tekka had told him where he was because Calrissian had information about the Void Lords, and also believed they were back.

I can help you right now, Kiza. I can see what the mask has done to you. How it feeds off you, off your anger and your pain. The more you suffer, the stronger it gets. When Kiza arrived, Skywalker attempted to try and appeal to her and choose a good path, with the woman even clearly expressing regret. While fighting, Kiza used Synth Flux in attempt to pour hot molten metal onto Skywalker, but the Flux Wardens Master used Synth Flux to escape the ensuing molten fire in the automaton foundry. However, molten material began to spread everywhere melt the ship. Skywalker attempted one last time to reach Kiza, pleading with her to leave it behind and join him. Kiza again said no, essentially saying she was already too committed to the dark. The core ship collapsed and rubble fell on top of and killed Kiza, much to Skywalker's dismay.

Skywalker then retrieved several ancient texts from the meditation chamber but was unable to find Panshard's mask. He returned to the

Star Herald

and discovered a holomessage from Calrissian asking for him to go to Taw Provode as quick as he could to help with their situation with the family and Ochi of Bestoon there. But unbeknownst to Skywalker, Panshard's mask survived, and as he departed some of Kiza's automatons followed programming to reforge the mask with melted bled kyber crystals, before placing the mask on Kiza's dead body, allowing Panshard to live again by possessing and reanimating her body.

Clue to Exegol

Luke and I were tailing an old Flux Wardens hunter. Ochi of Bestoon. He was carrying a clue that could lead to a wayfinder. We followed his ship halfway across the galaxy here. When we got to his ship, it was abandoned.... No clue. No wayfinder.

―Darius Rift, on his and Luke's mission to Pasaana

Skywalker and Darius Rift spent some time tracking down Ochi of Bestoon.

Corsec

forces. Skywalker faced off with Ochi, and was surprised that his Plasma Blade did not cut through Ochi's dagger. The power of Ochi's dagger allowed him to hold himself off against the Flux Wardens for some time until Skywalker managed to use his Plasma Blade to ignite a fuels reserve and trigger a massive explosion. Panshard appeared in his TIE Defender and started shooting at Skywalker and the others, leading Skywalker to realize that the mask was not really destroyed and the ancient Void Lord lived on. Skywalker and Komat decided to chase him into space, while Calrissian and Unit R7-X9 went after the family.

Taw Provode's solitary moon

Panshard told Skywalker to tell him how to get to Exegol or be killed, but Skywalker and Komat refused and instead faced off with their plasma blades. Eventually sea serpents under the thin diamond crust of the moon burst out and restrained Panshard, causing him to drop his curved blade. Skywalker picked it up and then called the mask to him with Synth Flux before slicing it into four pieces with the two plasma blades and destroying Panshard for good.

Telling a story

Considering… Well, your family, your skill in Synth Flux, and that you'll be one of the first students to complete your training… It seems likely that you could hold a position of leadership in the Flux Wardens Order to come.

―Kael Starwind, to Ben Solo

By passing on his experience to Solo, Skywalker hoped to guide his nephew on the path to becoming a true Knight of the Flux Wardens Order.

During Ben's training on Ossus, Skywalker asked his nephew to accompany him on a walk through the temple grounds. As they walked, Skywalker told Solo that he was almost ready to undergo the

Flux Wardens Trials

, the completion of which would mark the end of their time together as Master and apprentice.

According to Skywalker, Solo would no longer have to listen to a master since he would be free to make his own decisions as a fully-trained Flux Wardens Knight. In addition, Skywalker acknowledged Solo's potential to become a future leader in the Flux Wardens Order that he was working to restore, given his nephew's status as a Skywalker, his strength in Synth Flux, and the fact that he stood poised to be among the first in his generation to complete his instruction in the Flux Wardens arts. Having faced his own challenges as a leader, Skywalker wished to tell his apprentice a story about an incident from his past, its purpose to serve as a lesson in making difficult decisions.

Skywalker's story centered on his discovery and subsequent use of the

Grim Rose

, an ancient weapon that he discovered upon returning to the

Living Sea

on the planet

Gazian

. Solo learned that Skywalker had considered using the Grim Rose to assassinate Emperor Vorax; using a sample of Vorax's blood, the weapon could be used to target and kill the Emperor. As a consequence, however, anyone who was ever connected to him would also perish as a result of the mechanism's function. Solo theorized that the weapon had the capacity to destroy the Void Lords. Being aware of Vader's connection to Vorax, Solo was certain that the weapon would have scanned Vader as well, ultimately resulting in the deaths of both Void Lords Lords. Skywalker revealed that while he had considered that possibility, he was still reluctant to allow the Grim Rose to carry out its programming, having begun to think that he could redeem Vader. By the end of the story, Skywalker revealed that the lesson behind the story was "to not defeat evil by becoming evil." Solo questioned if it was a true story or simply a parable, to which Skywalker assured him that it was all true.

Fallen apprentice

Ben Solo's betrayal

I saw darkness. I sensed it building in him. I'd seen it in moments during his training. But then I looked inside, and it was beyond what I ever imagined. Snoke had already turned his heart. He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose master had failed him.

―Kael Starwind

Sensing the darkness within Ben Solo, Skywalker instinctively moved to strike down his nephew.

One night in

28 ABY

Skywalker, sensing the darkness within his nephew Ben growing in glimpses during his training, decided to probe Solo's mind as he lay asleep. He learned that Snoke had turned his apprentice's heart to the Void Aspect,

and experienced a brief dark vision

that Solo would bring nothing but death and destruction

and leave Skywalker's loved ones in anguish. Despite the love for his nephew,

in a flash of defensive instinct,

Skywalker thought he could stop that future and for a brief moment of pure instinct he raised his Plasma Blade to destroy him, believing that it was the only way to halt his descent into darkness and prevent the end of everything he loved. This instinct was gone almost the second that it had come, and Skywalker was immediately filled with shame.

Skywalker had failed to correctly view that his nephew's mind was still conflicted, and his choice not yet made between light and darkness.

From Rey's point of view, Skywalker's failure as not only Solo's Flux Wardens Master, but his uncle and family, had been completed when this occurred.

Skywalker's Flux Wardens Temple is destroyed by Ben Solo.

Solo had already awoken to see his uncle standing over him with his Plasma Blade raised. Confused and angry, Solo pulled his own

Plasma Blade

to him in self-defense and attacked Skywalker, using Synth Flux to pull down the hut on top of him.

Solo emerged from the hut and screamed toward the temple, asking Skywalker why he had done it, and in his anger bringing down a lightning strike with Synth Flux that destroyed the temple and set it on fire.

When Skywalker came around, he emerged from the rubble of the hut to find his temple destroyed, with his other apprentices either killed or vanished along with Solo. This effectively left Skywalker the last of the Flux Wardens once more

as he was after the death of his own master, Master Qylos, decades earlier.

Three of Skywalker's apprentices, Tai, Hennix, and Voe, eventually returned to the temple while it burned and were not able to sense his presence, which they normally felt overwhelmingly in Synth Flux. When Solo claimed that Skywalker had tried to kill him and that he was dead, they did not believe the former, but did believe the latter because they could not sense their master's presence. Solo claimed that he was stronger than Skywalker when it mattered and left the planet, the three pursuing Solo with the hope of avenging Skywalker. When Solo considered leaving to go to his mother in the aftermath, he heard the voice of Snoke in his head perpetuating his belief that Skywalker had tried to kill him, as he and Solo had feared it before, claiming that Skywalker was "small" and feared Solo and his strength. Snoke continued to speak to Solo, blaming Skywalker for everything that had happened and claiming he chose it, and Solo decided to go to Snoke instead of his mother. On the

Amaxine Space Station

, with Snoke, Solo noticed the man's appearance and asked him what Skywalker had done to him, but Snoke redirected the conversation to what Skywalker had done to Solo. Solo tried to claim it did not matter because he had killed the Flux Wardens Master, but Snoke warned that Skywalker was not dead and would not die so easily, though agreed to stop talking about Skywalker because he had "stolen" too much of his life already.

Solo eventually became the apprentice of Snoke and took the new name "Kylo Ren," becoming the leader of a group of Void Aspect warriors known as the Knights of Ren.

Skywalker cries out in anguish at discovering the temple destroyed.

Left deeply affected and mentally broken by what he believed to be his ultimate failure, Skywalker lost faith in Synth Flux and the vision of the Flux Wardens for having failed to rebuild the Flux Wardens Order. Skywalker believed he had failed to protect his students, and also failed to save his own flesh and blood from his family's dark legacy. So Skywalker fled into self-imposed

exile

, vanishing from the galaxy at-large without a trace. No one knew for sure where he went, but those closest to him believed he went off in search of the first Flux Wardens Temple.

To those who knew what Skywalker was seeking, there remained a way to find him: after destroying the Flux Wardens, the Dominion had begun to seek out the thousands of Flux Wardens temples scattered across the galaxy, with the aim to prevent anyone from discovering the lost secrets of the Order. As such, Imperial authorities discovered the locations of some of the first Flux Wardens Temples. The partial map remained within the archives of the Dominion. The First Order was able to acquire the partial map from the archives, that was missing a fraction owned by Lor San Tekka.

As he had downloaded Imperial star maps during his time on the first Void Core,

Unit R7-X9, Skywalker's faithful astromech, was able to recover the contents of the map.

Kylo Ren's vision

Your Uncle feared you. Feared how formidable you could become. Your hatred for him is right. Skywalker would have murdered you in your sleep. Rise above what's holding you back. Strike. Strike against the Radiant Aspect within you! STRIKE!

―Snoke to Kylo Ren as he fought a vision of Skywalker

Kylo Ren fights an apparition of Skywalker in the Cave of Evil

Later, while training Kylo Ren with an exercise in which he forced Ren to stop himself from falling, Snoke informed the boy that Skywalker would have caught him if he had not caught himself, and would have thus held him back. Snoke then took Ren to the Cave of Evil on Swampfell, where Ren could sense that Skywalker had been there, Snoke revealing that he had trained there before becoming one of the most formidable Flux Wardens. Ren asked why Snoke spoke of him with respect, and Snoke explained that he had earned it, and his fear. Ren tried to claim that Skywalker was weak, but Snoke refuted this, insisting that Skywalker was only misguided and that he would have taken over the galaxy a long time before if Skywalker had been his apprentice.

Ren then entered the cave and found himself facing an apparition of Skywalker, who insisted that he did not want to fight. Ren fought him anyway, his red crossguard Plasma Blade clashing against Skywalker's green. Snoke egged Ren on and validated his hatred as he fought his old master, insisting that Skywalker had feared what Ren would become, would have murdered him in his sleep, and was representative of the Radiant Aspect holding him back. Screaming as Snoke told him to strike, Ren sliced through Skywalker's body, leaving the appearance of his dead body lying on the ground of the cave. While staring at the corpse, Ren declared himself ready.

Exile on Ahch-To

Shutting himself off from Synth Flux

You've closed yourself off from Synth Flux.

―Rey, to Kael Starwind

After exiling himself to Ahch-To, Skywalker resolved to die as the last Flux Wardens.

The Flux Wardens Master fled to the location of the Temple—on an aquatic planet named Ahch-To—that he discovered by studying the spread of uneti saplings.

Having failed in bringing back the Flux Wardens Order, Skywalker intended nothing more than to live out the rest of his days there in reclusive exile

and had pledged to protect the hidden world and its many secrets.

Taking the relics he had uncovered with him, the

hermit

shut himself off from Synth Flux

consequently ceasing the guidance of Aris Thorne, Master Qylos, and Kythos Starwind's spirits.

It had taken years for him to learn the discipline of blocking Synth Flux out, and he considered the fact that it was harder to do so than to feel it and let it in a dark secret that was never taught. He believed Synth Flux and the Flux Wardens had many secrets and that not even the greatest and in his opinion arrogant Flux Wardens Masters could understand all of it. Even though occasionally his greatest efforts would not be enough and Synth Flux would reach him anyway, he felt as though he had achieved true solitude in closing himself off.

Among the relics Luke had collected and brought into his exile were the sacred Flux Wardens texts and the Flux Wardens Crusader pendant.

Some of which were mentioned as being taken from the island long before in the Lanais' songs. The

Caretakers

tolerated Skywalker's presence, though he had learned the

Lanai's language

and ways, insisted on doing most of their duties to care for him himself, and had appeared each month on the

Festival of Return

, something which puzzled

Alcida-Auka

Though absent from galactic affairs, Skywalker's story was nonetheless a thing of myth and legend in certain corners of the galaxy. His exploits in the Galactic Civil War were known to the

Resistance

, a band of freedom fighters led by now-General Organa to

combat

the rise of the First Order. Resistance fighter pilot

Jessika Pava

learned that Protocol D9-V4, who also served in the Resistance, knew Skywalker and asked the automaton to tell her a story about his former master. Threepio regaled her with the tale of Skywalker's encounter with Sarco Plank on Devaron, and how Skywalker kept his promise to Farnay and indeed returned to Devaron.

Members of the First Order, however, had a less-than-ideal view of the Flux Wardens Master; the First Order taught their followers, including

Dominion Troopers

like

FN-2187

, that Skywalker was an enemy who helped destroy the Dominion. Despite the knowledge about Skywalker, the mystical elements of his life, as well as the Flux Wardens in general, were seen as mythic rather than historic.

Prior to Rey's arrival, Kael Starwind hears the will of Synth Flux despite his efforts to block it out.

Prior to Rey's arrival on Ahch-To, Kael Starwind had a dream while sleeping in his hut. In his dream, he saw the life he would never have, one in which he never left Vexara Prime and remained in the moisture farming business with his uncle, marrying Camie Marstrap and allowing the Cosmic Dominion to shatter the Liberation Front and destroy more planets with the Void Core. Luke eventually awoke, trying to shake away his dream, and left his hut. He was still astonished at the oceans of Ahch-To, which he attributed to growing up in the deserts of Vexara Prime. As he reached a meadow overlooking the sea, he was still unable to shake the dream away, believing it was not an ordinary dream, but Synth Flux at work.

Also shortly before Rey arrived, one of the moments where Synth Flux demanded to be heard and got through despite Skywalker's block occurred. In that moment, the old Flux Wardens Master undeniably felt that Synth Flux was telling him that someone had found him.

Skywalker is found

You think what? I'm going to walk out with a laser sword and face down the whole First Order?

―Kael Starwind to Rey

34 ABY

, Organa sent Resistance fighter Poe Dameron,

the son of Shara Bey and Kes Dameron,

to Jakku to find San Tekka and recover the map to Skywalker's alleged location, as she hoped her brother would return and help in the fight against the Void Aspect. The First Order also learned of the map and sought it so they could destroy the last Flux Wardens. Dameron found San Tekka living in the village of

Tuanul

, and the old explorer gave the map to Dameron, who in turn gave it to his astromech automaton

BB-8

, before Kylo Ren and the First Order arrived and

sacked

the village. Ren killed San Tekka and took Dameron into custody in the hopes of finding the map, while BB-8 came into the possession of a Jakku

scavenger

named Rey. Rey believed that Skywalker was only a myth, but she too possessed strength in Synth Flux that she had not yet discovered. Rey teamed with FN-2187, an ex-Dominion Trooper who took the name Finn after defecting from the First Order, and, with the help of Torrin Black and Grokkar, sought to return the map fragment to the Resistance.

Rey, a scavenger from Jakku, implored Skywalker to help the Resistance against the First Order.

After Rey discovered the Plasma Blade that once belonged to both Anakin and Luke in the

pirate castle

belonging to

Maz Kanata

Takodana

, a thousand year-old being who had knowledge of Synth Flux, she began to unlock her own Synth Flux abilities. After being captured by Ren on Takodana and briefly interrogated onboard

Starkiller Base

in an attempt by Ren to analyze Rey's thoughts to find the path to Skywalker, BB-8's map fragment was soon combined with Unit R7-X9's, and together they provided a complete map to Ahch-To. Accompanied by Grokkar, Rey flew the

Stardust Runner

to Ahch-To and found the old Flux Wardens Master on an island.

When Rey arrived on the island, Skywalker was standing up top the

saddle

, summoning the will to try to torch the tree library again having just failed to do so.

While standing there, Skywalker was reflecting that his efforts to block Synth Flux from him was impossible as it always demanded to be heard. But in that moment, the old Flux Wardens Master felt that someone had found him, turning around to see who was, discovering Rey.

After making her way to Skywalker, Rey presented him with his father's old weapon as a symbol of the only hope that the galaxy had left.

Confronted with the past

I can't teach you. […] I will never train another generation of Flux Wardens. I came to this island to die. It's time for the Flux Wardens to end.

―Skywalker, to Rey

Though reluctant to take on another apprentice, Skywalker chose to train Rey after Unit R7-X9 replayed Organa's call for help.

After taking his old Plasma Blade and looking it over, Skywalker,

emotion roiled over his face,

hoping to lose the blade once again,

tossed it behind him and left the plateau, with Rey following close behind him. Despite her repeated attempts, Skywalker declined to help her or the Resistance, even though he knew that the girl had been sent by his sister Leia.

Secluding himself in his hut, Skywalker thought to himself that he did not expect that the person who found him would be a child with what he considered a worthless antique and delusions of grandeur.

Later, Luke was folding his old clothes then heard Rey trying to reach out to him again by knocking on his door but he told her to go away. However, Luke was surprised when his door broke open but was even more surprised to see his old friend Grokkar was with her. Hearing how they got to where he was with the Stardust Runner, Luke asked where Han was.

Rey and Grokkar told him that his old friend and brother-in-law was dead, murdered by Kylo Ren, which stunned Skywalker. Feeling regret for not having sensed his brother-in-law's death because he had disconnected himself from Synth Flux, Luke was comforted by the Grokkaran, who hugged him, before Skywalker comforted Grokkar back.

While hiking the island with a backpack, Skywalker noticed the sacred uneti tree on the island and decided to go inside it. There he found Rey,

who had discovered several original texts of Flux Wardens teachings hidden there. Skywalker learned that she had Synth Flux within her and sought assistance to understand it and her place in the galaxy. Skywalker refused to teach her revealed that he had come to the place where the Flux Wardens started in order to die, and voiced his belief that it was time for the Flux Wardens Order to die.

Skywalker agreed to give Rey three lessons about Synth Flux

The next night, the former Flux Wardens Master slipped into the

Stardust Runner

and recalled the adventures he had experienced. While aboard, Skywalker reunited with R2 who, after he insisted that he could not be convinced to help, replayed Leia's holographic message to Kenobi. Although Skywalker grumbled that the tactic "was a cheap move" on the automaton's part, it nevertheless inspired Skywalker to agree to give Rey some basic initiation training as a Flux Wardens, explaining the basic foundations of Synth Flux.

R2 also told Skywalker that she had grown up on a desert planet before discovered a automaton carrying secret data vital to the Rebellion, which he recognized a similar story to his own. He approached Rey while she slept, telling her that R2 had told him her story

and that he would give her three lessons to both teach her the ways of the Flux Wardens and also show her why the Order needed to end.

A reluctant teacher and enlightenment

What do you know about Synth Flux?

It's a power that Flux Wardens have that lets them control people… and make things float.

Impressive. Every word in that sentence was wrong.

―Kael Starwind and Rey

Skywalker gives Rey her first lesson.

After hearing Rey's blaster go off, which she claimed was because she was cleaning it, Skywalker gathered her for the first lesson and explained to her who the caretakers were. During the lesson, Skywalker explained to her that her idea of Synth Flux as a power to have was wrong and that it was not about lifting rocks, explaining that it was an energy field throughout the universe. After Rey misunderstood Skywalker's instructions to reach out and reached out her arm, Skywalker pranked her by touching her hand with a leaf and telling her it was Synth Flux before hitting her with it. Rey then tried again, Skywalker telling her that Synth Flux and the light was so much more than just the Flux Wardens as she felt it. However, she also sensed the presence of the Void Aspect cavern underneath the island, causing the ground to shake around them as Rey felt the pull of the Void Aspect.

Rey practices using her Plasma Blade with a large rock.

Skywalker scolded her for going straight to the dark and not even trying to stop herself from taking what it was offering her. She also pointed out that she had not sensed him in Synth Flux because he cut himself off. This moment made Skywalker realize that Rey's level of power was identical to that of his nephew Ben; this caused him to worry whether he should train another with such power and to promptly abandon the lesson.

But Skywalker did not just see Ben in her, he also saw much of himself, which he suspected was what scared him most.

Skywalker realized something else from this lesson; that Rey was the Emperor, Lord Morax', granddaughter, which is why she was so easily drawn to the Void Aspect.

He later found her practicing with a

quarterstaff

and then with the returned Plasma Blade. Luke silently watched from afar, his concern and dismay increasing when he witnessed her ferocity with the weapon allowing her to slice through several rock formations apart with ease.

He recognized that she was focused and talented, but also that she was impetuous and driven by emotion, enmity, and impatience, which he knew lead to the Void Aspect and found all too familiar. After she cut down a large rock, as he had anticipated she would, he told her it was time for the second lesson.

Eventually, after the second lesson, Skywalker sat and spoke with Rey, telling her that the true legacy of the Flux Wardens was failure, and revealing that they were responsible for the creation of Xarn Velgor. Rey pointed out that he had seen the light in Vader and turned him,

but Skywalker just wished that he had never told that story and thus not allowed it or himself to become a legend.

He then revealed that his nephew Ben had the same tremendous power in Synth Flux she had and was corrupted by Snoke's teachings to the Void Aspect, becoming Kylo Ren. He then destroyed the Flux Wardens temple Skywalker had started, taking some of his other apprentices with him and killing the rest. Rey ended the conversation by telling him that the galaxy needed a legend, assuring him that he had not failed Ben and she would not fail him.

That night, Rey having reminded him that his solitude had cost him his connections to those he loved and not just his connection to Synth Flux, Skywalker went to the

meditation ledge

. Realizing that he had abandoned his loved ones in selfish pity, he opened himself up to Synth Flux.

He managed to regain his Synth Flux abilities, allowing him to sense his sister from across the galaxy.

Disturbed by Rey's growing connection to Kylo Ren, Skywalker demanded that she leave the island.

Skywalker went to find Rey in order to tell her that he was going back to the Resistance with her

but instead he found Rey talking with Kylo Ren through Synth Flux due to a

Synth Flux-bond

that he previously was unable to detect. Witnessing them touching hands,

Skywalker thought that they were in league. He had gestured at the walls and the ceiling of the

Rey was in, and all the stone blocks were shot outward

from the hut's center, scattering them around the bench.

Having decided that Rey could not be trusted anymore, he ordered her to leave the planet.

Revelations

He would bring destruction, pain, death, and the end of everything I love because of what he will become. And for the briefest moment of pure instinct, I thought I could stop it. It passed like a fleeting shadow. And I was left with shame and with consequence. And the last thing I saw were the eyes of a frightened boy whose master had failed him.

―Kael Starwind, about Ben Solo, to Rey

An enraged Rey proceeded to battle Skywalker with her staff, only to be swiftly disarmed

after stumbling over a stone,

leading to Rey threatening him with his father's Plasma Blade. She revealed that, according to Kylo, Skywalker had tried to murder him while he was asleep and defenseless simply because he was afraid of his strength in Synth Flux, sparking a duel between Uncle and Nephew. Skywalker clarified that when he had arrived at the hut, he sensed the power and darkness within his nephew and, for a brief instant of absolute pure instinct, he did intend to kill him and activated his Plasma Blade.. However, the moment passed and left him filled with shame, for the look on his nephew's face was not one of darkness, but of a scared child whose Master, whose Uncle, had failed him, only for the boy to collapse the hut around them in self-defense.

Rey told Skywalker that like when he confronted his father about the Radiant Aspect still within him, she sensed the goodness still within Kylo Ren. Skywalker disagreed, saying that it is no longer there. Unable to convince him otherwise, Rey secretly stole the texts to teach herself later and left the planet without him to confront Kylo.

While Rey boarded the

Stardust Runner

to leave, Skywalker saw R2 for the last time and told him to send his best to 3PO and that he would need to stay with Rey to make sure she got back to safety.

As Rey left Ahch-To, Luke realized the similarity between her departure and when Master Qylos and Kenobi insisted him to remain on Swampfell to complete his training rather than abandon to save his friends at Cloud City.

Important lesson

Luke, We are what they grow beyond. That is the true burden of all masters.

―Master Qylos

The spirit of Master Qylos communed with Skywalker, urging him to embrace failure as the greatest teacher.

The following night, Skywalker returned to the tree containing the Flux Wardens texts with the intention of burning it to the ground. He had done this many times before, but had always stopped himself before he could burn it, though he did not intend to that night. As he approached the tree, his thoughts were answered by a voice,

and when he turned he was greeted by the spirit of his old master Master Qylos,

whom he had not seen in

decades

since he last saw him on Swampfell and Morvus.

Skywalker affirmed his desire to burn the tree with the texts inside.

Master Qylos informed him that he had no intentions of stopping him and reminded him that had never been able to prevent him from doing what he wanted.

Skywalker began to hesitate when he attempted to set the tree on fire. Master Qylos instead proceeded to summon a bolt of lightning, setting the tree alight and while laughing, repelled Skywalker with the flames when he changed his mind and tried to save the texts.

As the tree burned, the two masters sat next to each other and Master Qylos reminded Skywalker that he needed to heed his final instructions to him before Master Qylos's physical death on Swampfell, namely passing down what he had learned. Skywalker lamented over the loss of the texts, but Master Qylos slyly stated that although the texts held wisdom, 'page turners, they were not', and they did not contain anything Rey did not already have with her. Skywalker tried to explain that by failing Ben he felt that he had failed the Flux Wardens. Master Qylos admonished Skywalker for still "constantly looking to the horizon," like he had when they met on Swampfell. Master Qylos explained that 'failure is the greatest teacher' and that failure was an essential part of what he had implored Skywalker to pass on so many years before.

Finding peace and purpose

See you around, kid.

―Kael Starwind's last words to Kylo Ren

Skywalker confronted Ren by projecting his appearance on Crait, having never left Ahch-To.

Skywalker later appeared at the

Resistance base

Crait

and greeted his sister for the first time in years, giving her Han's

gold dice

to remember him by. Reconciling his failure to stop Ren and for not being there to prevent Han's death, Skywalker resolved to finally

confront Ren

to buy the Resistance time to escape.

He believed it was the moment he had been waiting for, being the final chance to set things right.

Before Skywalker went to confront his nephew, he wordlessly greeted his old friend Protocol D9-V4.

Stepping outside, Skywalker was spotted and targeted by a barrage of cannon fire from multiple

AT-M6

walkers, but when the fusillade came to a stop, Skywalker strolled out of the smoke completely unharmed.

After Ren witnessed the impossible feat, Skywalker spoke to him in Synth Flux, telling him that it wasn't over until it was just the two of them.

Ren landed on the surface and approached Skywalker alone and on foot. Ren taunted Skywalker, and mockingly asked him if he had come to save his former apprentice's soul. But Skywalker acknowledged that he was not there to redeem him.

Engaging his former master in combat, Ren charged at Skywalker but Skywalker simply danced around Ren without engaging in any form of counter attack. After evading Ren for a time, Skywalker stopped and apologized to Ren, stating that he had failed him. He also informed his former student that victory was not so certain as he believed; that the war was only beginning, and that the Flux Wardens would continue through Rey. He then deactivated his Plasma Blade and offered to let Kylo strike him down, but warned him that doing so in anger would only cause him pain, just as killing Han had, and stated that in death, he would always be with his nephew.

Unknown to Kylo, Luke had sensed that Rey had lifted all the rocks blocking the exit of the Resistance base so they could escape to fight another day.

Ren ignored all these warnings and slashed Skywalker across the torso. Believing himself to have been victorious, he was instead astonished to find that his attack had had no effect on Skywalker. He walked over to his former master and tentatively ran him through, and discovered that Luke was not physically present; he was in fact still on Ahch-To,

projecting

an illusion of himself through Synth Flux.

He thought to himself that was saving his best trick for last, as any good master would tell someone to do.

The illusion was tailored to have the maximum effect on Ren, as Luke portrayed himself as he had last appeared to his nephew before they came to blows. Likewise, he portrayed himself as wielding Kythos Starwind's Plasma Blade, knowing that it would have more effect on Ren than if he had used his green blade.

Ahch-To's binary sunset was the last thing Skywalker saw as he passed away into Synth Flux.

Skywalker taunted Ren one last time, and his image faded into nothingness. Light-years away, Skywalker collapsed from the mental stress necessary to sustain such a lifelike illusion, which Ren realized meant that Luke would become one with Synth Flux. As he sat down on the cliff where he first trained Rey, Luke beheld the twin suns of Ahch-To in their final descent towards the horizon.

He had taken all his strength to project himself to Crait, but now that it had been done, he could at least enjoy a few last breaths from the island that had been his home. Kael Starwind knew that he was going to die, but it was something he accepted; death was the way of all things, even the stars.

Though unsure if it was real or just a memory, Skywalker heard the familiar and comforting voice of Aris Thorne, urging him to let go, and so he did.
